# Cantrip tracker

Generated by `rust_syntax/normalize/tools/cantrip_tree.py tracker`. Do not edit: the classes come from
`data/cantrip_requirements.json`, the rest from what the generator emits.

Against Cantrip 01aba4b159: 33529 records, 27915 whole, 5566 with holes, 48 with no card face.
Of those with holes Cantrip has written 202 by hand, which leaves 5364.

A card with holes has its file all the same. Each `planned!` in it names a requirement below.
`cards` counts what Cantrip does not have yet. `written` counts the cards Cantrip has written by hand:
at least some of what that row asks can be done there, so it may be a gap in the generator, not in the engine.

A requirement is one form the generator could not write, not one piece of work, and `class` comes from the form's
family, not from anyone having tried. `fix` is from `data/cantrip_audit.json`, where each form's verdict cites the API
or a hand-written card. It comes from reading the code: nothing there has been compiled or played.

## Counters a card brings with it

169 counters are a name and nothing more (CR 122.1), and 319 cards use them. They are not gaps: the card's file uses the kind, and a commit just before the card adds the line to `counter_kinds!`.

## Text the source rewrites

The source changes 112 passages of card text on purpose before parsing (`grammars/find_replace.json5`), and Cantrip plays Oracle text, so each has a verdict in `data/cantrip_rewrites.json`. 0 have none yet. Those that change nothing or sit on cards not played are not listed.

| cards | verdict | note |
|---|---|---|
| Vampirism | reading | No ruling; the only reading in which "Other" does anything on an Aura. |
| Sensei Golden-Tail | undone | Oracle text says training; read.rs names the counter Training. |
| Call a Surprise Witness | reading | Rulings on Chainer and Ascent of the Worthy have the creature enter with the type; Cantrip's hand-written card adds it after. |
| Chaos Mutation | undone | One slot in Oracle text, one per player in the rewrite; target_counts.rs writes Oracle's slot. |
